**Preconditions.** An account the attacker controls; on pubnet an API key. **Impact.** Real XLM per event, otherwise bounded only by the hourly budgets.

**Mitigations**

- A payer whose settlements were included and failed PAYER_FAILURES_PER_HOUR times in the last hour is refused with a coded, retryable reason until the hour moves on (3 by default on pubnet). ([`packages/facilitator/src/limits.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/src/limits.ts), `failuresPerHour`)
- Pubnet's expiration margin defaults to two ledgers, so a settlement that lands a ledger late does not fail after the fee is paid. ([`apps/rail402/src/config.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/config.ts), `PUBNET_EXPIRATION_MARGIN_LEDGERS`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/facilitator/test/limits.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/test/limits.test.ts): puts a payer whose settlements fail on chain into a cooldown
- [`apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts): defaults the pubnet expiration margin to two ledgers

**Residual risk.** The first failures per payer per hour are paid; new payer accounts cost the attacker reserves. An exact failure that leaves the payer's authorization usable counts once its settlement finishes, when the authorization expired unused (at most maxTimeoutSeconds later); until then the hourly fee budget bounds what such failures cost.

### S10. Surge pricing against the inclusion floor and the fee ceilings

Mitigated · Denial of service · boundary TB3 (Facilitator and Stellar) · asset: Availability

**Threat.** During mainnet surge pricing the inclusion bid sits at its floor or cap and is never included, or a ceiling that silently defaults lets one settlement cost far more than the operator planned.

**What Stellar makes different.** Mainnet's Soroban inclusion fees have sat at 200 stroops; the bid falls back to the floor when fee stats cannot be read.

**Preconditions.** Network congestion, or an RPC that cannot serve getFeeStats. **Impact.** Settlements pend and time out, channels stay leased; or each settlement spends up to an unplanned ceiling.

**Mitigations**

- Pubnet refuses to start without explicit PUBNET_MAX_TX_FEE_STROOPS and PUBNET_INCLUSION_FEE_STROOPS of at least 200 (unless explicitly overridden) and, with upto, PUBNET_UPTO_MAX_TX_FEE_STROOPS. ([`apps/rail402/src/config.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/config.ts), `PUBNET_INCLUSION_FEE_STROOPS`)
- A metric counts fee-stats readings whose bid is pinned at the cap or bid blind at the floor, for alerting. ([`apps/rail402/src/metrics.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/metrics.ts), `inclusionFeePinned`)

**Tests**

- [`apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts): refuses pubnet without explicit fee ceilings and an inclusion floor of at least 200
- [`packages/facilitator/test/fees.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/test/fees.test.ts): reports when the bid is pinned at the cap or fee stats are unreadable

**Residual risk.** In a sustained surge above the cap, pubnet settlements pend or fail; that is the configured trade-off.

### S11. Concurrent admissions overshoot the hourly budget

Mitigated · Denial of service · boundary TB4 (Facilitator and its state) · asset: Sponsor XLM

**Threat.** Many settlements are admitted at once while the committed fees are just under the budget; each passes the check before any of them records its envelope.

**What Stellar makes different.** n/a (facilitator logic).

**Preconditions.** A burst of concurrent settles near the budget edge, on one or several replicas. **Impact.** Spend above the budget by up to the number of concurrent admissions times the fee ceiling.

**Mitigations**

- Each claim reserves its settlement's fee ceiling until its envelope is recorded, and the budget check and the claim are one step under a per-network database lock shared by every replica. ([`packages/store-postgres/src/settlement-ledger.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/store-postgres/src/settlement-ledger.ts), `BUDGET_LOCK_ID`)
- The engine passes the reservation and the hourly limits with every sponsored claim; an exact settlement reserves its worst case, the fee ceiling plus the highest inclusion bid its fee bump pays again. ([`packages/facilitator/src/engine.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/src/engine.ts), `exactReserveStroops`)
- The sponsor refuses to sign a fee bump whose fee exceeds the settlement's reservation, so nothing broadcast can cost more than was reserved. ([`packages/facilitator/src/engine.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/src/engine.ts), `costs more than the settlement reserved`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/facilitator/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/src/testing.ts): never lets concurrent claims at the budget edge exceed the budget
- [`packages/facilitator/test/fee-exposure.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/test/fee-exposure.integration.test.ts): stops settling once the hourly fee budget is committed, before any RPC work
- [`packages/facilitator/test/fee-reservation.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/test/fee-reservation.test.ts): reserves the fee ceiling plus one inclusion bid, the fee bump's worst case
- [`packages/facilitator/test/fee-reservation.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/test/fee-reservation.test.ts): refuses to sign a fee bump that costs more than the settlement reserved

**Residual risk.** None: a settlement costs at most its reservation.

### S12. Settlements admitted while the sponsor balance is unknown

Mitigated · Denial of service · boundary TB3 (Facilitator and Stellar) · asset: Sponsor XLM

**Threat.** After a restart or during an RPC outage the sponsor balance is unknown and the reserve floor is skipped, so settlements spend into the reserve that keeps channels and the authority alive.

**What Stellar makes different.** Channel and authority reserves are sponsored by the sponsor; dropping below its own minimum balance makes fee bumps fail mid-settlement.

**Preconditions.** A restart, or an RPC that cannot be read. **Impact.** Fees spent below the reserve floor.

**Mitigations**

- On pubnet the sponsor guard refuses with a retryable code until a balance has been read; testnet keeps admitting. ([`packages/facilitator/src/guard.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/src/guard.ts), `refuseWhileUnknown`)
- Pubnet is held, refusing its own settlements, while the sponsor balance is unknown or below its reserve. ([`apps/rail402/src/runtime.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/runtime.ts), `sponsor_balance_unknown`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/facilitator/test/fees.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/test/fees.test.ts): refuses on pubnet while the sponsor balance is unknown
- [`apps/rail402/test/network-isolation.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/network-isolation.integration.test.ts): serves pubnet once its RPC answers again, without a restart

**Residual risk.** None beyond a short refusal window after start.

### S13. Contract TTL upkeep spends outside the hourly budget

Mitigated · Denial of service · boundary TB3 (Facilitator and Stellar) · asset: Sponsor XLM

**Threat.** The upto TTL keeper extends or restores contract state with the sponsor's XLM; a lying RPC, a mis-sized ceiling or a loop makes it spend up to its ceiling repeatedly.

**What Stellar makes different.** TTL extension cost scales with entry size and ledgers; extending the code entry by the network maximum cost about 32 XLM on testnet.

**Preconditions.** The keeper enabled on pubnet; a bad simulation or a planning bug. **Impact.** Up to the keeper's ceiling per round, outside any settlement budget.

**Mitigations**

- Pubnet requires an explicit keeper ceiling, and a daily cap (one maximal extension per day unless configured) stops rounds that could pass it. ([`apps/rail402/src/upto-keeper.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/upto-keeper.ts), `dailyMaxFeeStroops`)
- Each keeper transaction reserves its fee ceiling as sponsor spend before it is signed, within the daily cap and the network's hourly budget, atomically with settlement claims; the reservation is corrected to the fee charged once known, and stays at the ceiling while a transaction that failed to confirm may still land. ([`packages/store-postgres/src/settlement-ledger.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/store-postgres/src/settlement-ledger.ts), `reserveSponsorSpend`)
- A transaction included and failed is counted at the fee it was charged; one with an unknown outcome keeps its ceiling counted. ([`apps/rail402/src/upto-keeper.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/upto-keeper.ts), `settleFailed`)

**Tests**

- [`apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts): requires explicit upto fee ceilings on pubnet, including the TTL keeper's
- [`apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts): caps the pubnet TTL keeper's daily spend at one extension unless configured
- [`apps/rail402/test/upto-archival.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/upto-archival.integration.test.ts): stops extending once the daily keeper cap is spent
- [`apps/rail402/test/upto-keeper-spend.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/upto-keeper-spend.integration.test.ts): keeps the ceiling counted when an extension's outcome is unknown, so the daily cap holds
- [`apps/rail402/test/upto-keeper-spend.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/upto-keeper-spend.integration.test.ts): waits when its ceiling would pass the network's hourly fee budget
- [`apps/rail402/test/upto-keeper-spend.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/upto-keeper-spend.integration.test.ts): records the fee charged once an extension lands, not its ceiling
- [`packages/facilitator/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/facilitator/src/testing.ts): reserves sponsor spend within its caps before submission and corrects it afterwards

**Residual risk.** One extension per day up to the ceiling, whatever its outcome on chain.

{/* generated:end */}

## Catalog poisoning

The Bazaar catalogs resources automatically after a successful settlement. The settlement proves one thing,
that a payment reached `payTo`; everything else in the resource description came from the buyer's request and
must be confirmed at the resource's own origin before it is published.

{/* generated:begin topic-catalog-poisoning */}

### P1. Forged listing metadata or price

Mitigated · Spoofing, Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A buyer (or a seller's buggy client) echoes a resource block with a false description, price or schema in the payment, hoping the catalog publishes it.

**What Stellar makes different.** Nothing on chain describes the resource; the settlement proves only that a payment to payTo happened. The metadata must be confirmed at its origin.

**Preconditions.** One real settlement to the seller. **Impact.** Misleading listings shown to buyers and agents.

**Mitigations**

- An HTTP resource, or an MCP tool at an http(s) endpoint, is published only after its own 402 (for a tool, the answer to an unpaid tools/call over MCP) confirms payTo, price and metadata; what is published always comes from that answer, so echoed metadata never changes a listing on its own. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `checkOrigins`)
- MCP tools are asked through safe-fetch with the MCP client: the tool must be declared in tools/list and its unpaid call must answer with the x402 PaymentRequired. ([`packages/bazaar/src/mcp-origin.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/mcp-origin.ts), `probeMcpTool`)
- A published listing is decided by one answer only, at its resource or its route template's example path, asked as its origin confirmed it, and a published MCP tool by its listed call; an answer at a path a payer chose never changes or withdraws it, a published tool whose listed call fails for now is retried without calling it with a payload's arguments, and an example kept from before examples were confirmed never withdraws it. While a listing not yet listed has a check open, the check keeps up to 4 paths per asset (the largest payments) and 8 in all (the latest) that its owner's payers paid, asks them largest payments first within its host's budget, going on from what earlier runs found when the budget runs out, and lists the listing from an answer that lists it for its owner at that answer's price first. Later settlements never reset a check's retries; one that pays the owner of a listing not yet listed makes its check due now and has its path asked again, and a run it makes due before the next scheduled retry spends none. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `decide`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): does not let echoed metadata change a listing until the origin confirms it
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lists an HTTP resource whose own response is not a 402
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): quarantines a listing whose origin does not name its owner
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists an MCP tool at an https endpoint only once its unpaid call confirms it
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): quarantines a tool its server does not sell to the listed payTo, with a coded reason
- [`packages/bazaar/test/mcp-origin.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/mcp-origin.test.ts): reads a tool's PaymentRequired from its unpaid call, sending the example arguments
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): asks only a route-template listing's example path whether it changed or still sells
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a claim's path change what a published route-template listing says
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): checks a new route-template listing at the path that created it, whatever a dust payment to
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets dust decide where a listing created with dust is checked, once a buyer paid its price
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): decide a listing that dust once had withdrawn
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a payment above
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets dust in many assets keep a later buyer's path from a new listing's check
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never withdraws or rewrites a listing whose unconfirmed example does not answer for its owner
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never resets a published listing's retries, whatever settlements follow
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists a listing paid at several paths while its origin was down, whatever the host's budget
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists a buyer's path before dust at other paths, whatever the host's budget
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists a buyer's path whatever dust in other assets and a claim ask of the host's budget
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists a tool from its buyer's call whatever failing calls dust proposed, whatever the host's budget
- [`packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts): goes on from the paths earlier runs found refused, never asking one twice
- [`packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts): asks a buyer's path before listing from a payment below the price at another
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists a listing waiting for a retry at its owner's next settlement once its origin is back
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): asks a buyer's path again at the next buyer's settlement after it answered without a 402
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never spends a pending listing's retries on runs its owner's settlements make due before the next

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `forged-metadata`, `live-bazaar` case `not-x402`.

**Residual risk.** A seller can describe its own resource falsely; buyers bind the price and recipient of a listing when paying (M6). Tools under an mcp:// URL have no origin to check (P10). Dust can still push a buyer's payment out of a new listing's open check: 4 payments in its asset at least as large, or 8 payments in other assets, two or more of them, made after it; the listing is then decided by the paths kept, and the buyer's next settlement adds its path again. When a host's budget is below a check's paths, a larger payment in any token is asked before the buyer's, which delays the buyer's path by about a minute for each budget's worth of such payments.

### P2. Taking over another seller's listing

Mitigated · Spoofing, Elevation of privilege · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** An attacker pays a URL that belongs to another seller, but to its own payTo, to take over or modify that listing.

**What Stellar makes different.** The settled payTo is the only identity the facilitator has proven, from a settlement that moved value to it. An upto zero moves nothing and proves nothing, so it never catalogs.

**Preconditions.** A settlement for the victim's URL to the attacker's account. **Impact.** Payments for a listed resource redirected to the attacker.

**Mitigations**

- A listing is bound to the settled payTo; another payTo is refused with bazaar_owner_conflict, and ownership moves only when the origin names the new payTo. ([`packages/bazaar/src/codes.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/codes.ts), `bazaar_owner_conflict`)
- A settlement that moved nothing (an upto zero, on-chain or off) never catalogs. A settlement that moved one base unit does not help either: a listing is published only with what its own origin answers (P1, P9). ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `unprovenSettlement`)
- Every claimant of a listing is kept until a check ran with it, a check only claims asked for never withdraws a published listing, and a claim is weighed at the listing's resource or route-template example path alone, never at the path the claimant paid: the listing moves only to a claimant that path names, and not its owner. A template's example is the path its creating settlement paid, then the one its origin confirmed when it was listed, never pathParams a payer echoed; any other answer there refuses the claim. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `mayWithdraw`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a different payTo modify a listing
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): transfers ownership only when the origin names the new payTo
- [`apps/rail402/test/bazaar.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/bazaar.integration.test.ts): refuses a different payTo for the same resource, with a coded reason
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): does not catalog a settlement that moved nothing, however high its cap
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): keeps every payTo's claim to a listing, so one claimant cannot replace another's
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never withdraws a published listing on a check only another payTo's claim asked for
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): moves a route-template listing only to a payTo its example path names too
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): takes a route-template listing's example from the path its origin confirmed, never from an echo
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lets a seller move its route-template listing to a new payTo when a payer's echoed example
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets an echoed example keep a seller from claiming a route template no origin confirmed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never moves a route-template listing to a claimant while its example path cannot be asked
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never moves a listing a tenant's buyer created, withdrawn before it was listed, to another tenant
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): to a claimant at another tenant's path
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a payment above a buyer's, at the claimant's path, move a new listing to the claimant
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a claim and a dust payment move a new route-template listing to the claimant's path
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): asks a route template's confirmed example spelled as it was confirmed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): weighs a claim on a listing not yet listed at its example spelled as it was paid

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `owner-conflict`.

**Residual risk.** A tool under an mcp:// URL is scoped to its owner, so it cannot be taken over, but its first content is whatever its first payer echoed (P10). On a host that serves one tenant per path, whoever first pays for a route template no origin has confirmed chooses its first example path, which the origin's answer there then decides, as it could by paying its own payTo. Listings cataloged before examples came from the paid path keep the example their first payer echoed until they are listed again. Such a kept example weighs claims all the same: when it is another tenant's path on a host that serves one tenant per path, that tenant's claim moves the listing to it; and while it answers for no payTo (an echoed path the seller does not sell), the listing stays with its owner, so a seller that moved to a new payTo cannot move it until that path answers or the listing expires.

### P3. Hostile JSON Schema in listing metadata

Mitigated · Denial of service, Information disclosure · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Availability

**Threat.** Metadata carries a schema with a catastrophic regular expression, a combinatorial check, deep nesting or an external $ref.

**What Stellar makes different.** None: this is a property of the catalog, which validates schemas it receives in payments.

**Preconditions.** One settlement carrying the schema. **Impact.** Catalog workers stalled, or the service fetching arbitrary URLs.

**Mitigations**

- Schemas compile and validate in a worker with a time budget and a bounded queue; external references are refused. ([`packages/bazaar/src/schema-sandbox.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/schema-sandbox.ts), `SchemaSandbox`)
- One sandbox per network and lane (settlements and origin checks, /verify previews), each with its own worker and queue; a busy validator decides nothing, and a settlement's cataloging stays queued and is retried. ([`packages/bazaar/src/schema-sandbox.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/schema-sandbox.ts), `SchemaSandboxes`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): stops a catastrophic-backtracking pattern within its budget and keeps serving
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): stops a combinatorial uniqueItems check within its budget
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): refuses oversized and deeply nested documents before compiling them
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): finds external references in every schema position, and only there
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): refuses validations beyond its queue at once instead of delaying everyone
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): validates each network's settlements and its previews in their own lanes, so a flood of slow schemas starves no one else
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): retries a settlement whose schema validation found the validator busy, instead of dropping it

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `schema-rejected`.

**Residual risk.** None.

### P4. Encoded traversal in a route template

Mitigated · Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A routeTemplate hides ../, NUL, CR/LF or a backslash behind percent-encoding so that a listing matches, or later fills, paths it should not.

**What Stellar makes different.** Nothing in the parsing is Stellar-specific. What a template changes is which paths are paid to one Stellar account: a listing and every path its template claims stay bound to the payTo proven by the settled transfer (P2), so a template can only widen a seller's own listing, and only over paths its own route matched.

**Preconditions.** One settlement carrying the template. **Impact.** A listing that claims paths of another resource, or a buyer tool that fills a path outside the seller's route.

**Mitigations**

- Templates are percent-decoded before the safety check, and used only when they match the paid path segment by segment. ([`packages/bazaar/src/url.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/url.ts), `isSafeTemplate`)
- Buyers refuse traversal when filling route parameters. ([`packages/sdk/src/listing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/sdk/src/listing.ts), `expandRoute`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): refuses route templates that decode to NUL, CR, LF, a backslash or an empty segment
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): uses a routeTemplate only when it is safe and matches the paid path
- [`packages/sdk/test/discovery.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/sdk/test/discovery.test.ts): fills route parameters and refuses traversal

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `route-template`.

**Residual risk.** None.

### P5. Wash settlements to flood or rank the catalog

Accepted risk · Tampering, Denial of service · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A seller pays itself through a second account many times to create many listings or to look popular.

**What Stellar makes different.** Settlements are cheap on Stellar, so volume is a weak signal of quality.

**Preconditions.** Two accounts and a little USDC. **Impact.** Catalog spam and manipulated rankings.

**Mitigations**

- New listings are rate-limited per owner and origin, per payer and for the whole catalog. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `maxNewListingsPerPayerPerHour`)
- The owner's and the network's hourly quotas are spent when a listing is first published: one its origin confirms while either is spent waits, without spending a retry, so listings created at once or kept pending for hours never publish past the quotas. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `newListingsSpent`)
- Tools no origin can confirm (mcp://) are counted apart, within half of the network's hourly quota of new listings and half of its room, so dust payments for them never use up what sellers with an origin need. ([`packages/bazaar/src/policy.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/policy.ts), `unverifiedBudget`)
- Search never ranks by settlement count: ties break by origin verification, then listing age. ([`packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts), `trustRank`)
- Self-payments are not cataloged. ([`packages/bazaar/src/codes.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/codes.ts), `bazaar_self_payment`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): bounds how many listings one payTo may create per hour
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): bounds the new listings one payer's settlements may create per hour
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): rejects self-payments
- [`packages/search/test/search.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/search/test/search.test.ts): serves filter-only queries by trust, then listing age, never by settlements bought
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): publishes no more new listings per hour than its network's quota, however many wait for their origin
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): counts a listing in the hour it is first published, however long ago it was created
- [`packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts): publishes listings staged pending past the hour only as the hourly quotas have room
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets tools no origin can confirm use up the hourly quota of listings an origin confirms
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets tools no origin can confirm take the room listings an origin confirmed need

**Residual risk.** A patient seller can still create listings up to the limits; each needs a working origin that answers 402, or, on testnet, takes a place among the mcp:// tools, which share half of the network's quota and room.

### P6. One payment used for many listings

Mitigated · Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** The same settled transaction is presented with different resource blocks to catalog several resources.

**What Stellar makes different.** A transaction hash identifies exactly one settlement on chain.

**Preconditions.** One settlement. **Impact.** Listings without payments behind them.

**Mitigations**

- Each settlement transaction catalogs at most one resource (a unique index on the observation's transaction). ([`packages/store-postgres/migrations/0005_catalog_integrity.sql`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/store-postgres/migrations/0005_catalog_integrity.sql), `listing_observations_transaction`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): catalogs at most one resource per settlement
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): records a repeated settlement once and never duplicates the listing
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): does not catalog a payment settled without a transaction

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `settlement-reused`, `live-bazaar` case `duplicate-settlement`.

**Residual risk.** None.

### P7. Injection through reasons and response headers

Mitigated · Tampering, Denial of service · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Availability

**Threat.** Metadata crafted so that a rejection reason quoting it breaks headers, logs or clients, or grows EXTENSION-RESPONSES beyond what proxies accept.

**What Stellar makes different.** EXTENSION-RESPONSES travels in the same /settle answer as the settled transaction hash. The payment is final on the ledger whatever happens to the header, so a header that breaks the answer would hide a settled payment from the seller; the bound keeps that answer intact.

**Preconditions.** One settlement with crafted metadata. **Impact.** Broken settle responses or log injection.

**Mitigations**

- Reasons are bounded and stripped of control characters; EXTENSION-RESPONSES stays within 4 KB. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `boundedReason`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): bounds a rejection reason that quotes the buyer's input
- [`packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/memory-catalog.test.ts): stays within the header budget whatever the outcome carries

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `header-bounded`.

**Residual risk.** None.

### P8. Duplicate listings through URL spellings

Mitigated · Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A seller or attacker lists one resource many times by varying case, ports, dot segments or percent-encoding.

**What Stellar makes different.** A spelling variant would be a second key with no owner, escaping the binding to the payTo proven on chain (P2). Listings are keyed by the canonical URL per network (stellar:testnet, stellar:pubnet), so one resource has one owner per network.

**Preconditions.** Settlements for the variant URLs. **Impact.** Duplicate results, and a second listing that escapes the owner binding of the first.

**Mitigations**

- URLs are canonicalised (case, default ports, dot segments, percent-encoding, query and fragment) before they key a listing. ([`packages/bazaar/src/url.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/url.ts), `canonicalizeUrl`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): canonicalises case, default ports, dot segments, query and fragment
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): normalizes percent-encoding so equivalent paths are one resource
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): treats equivalent spellings of one URL as one listing

**Residual risk.** None.

### P9. Planting a seller's MCP tool listing

Mitigated · Spoofing, Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A payer pays a seller's payTo one base unit (or settles one unit under an upto cap of any size) while echoing the seller's MCP endpoint and tool name with its own description, input schema or price, hoping to publish, freeze or domain-verify that content under the seller's identity, or to use up the seller's quota of new listings.

**What Stellar makes different.** The settlement proves a payment to payTo, not who wrote the echoed tool description. Dust costs the payer almost nothing and the sponsor pays the fee, so a settlement alone cannot vouch for content.

**Preconditions.** A trustline and one base unit of the asset; the seller's MCP endpoint and tool name. **Impact.** Spoofed tool descriptions (prompt injection) and prices shown to agents under the seller's verified identity; the seller's own tools not cataloged.

**Mitigations**

- A tool at an http(s) endpoint stays pending until the tool itself answers an unpaid tools/call with a PaymentRequired naming the owner; until an origin check has confirmed it, the tool must also offer the scheme, network, asset, payTo and price (cap) of a settlement for it, the one that created it or one its check keeps, or the listing is quarantined with origin_payment_mismatch. A later settlement never narrows what an earlier one proved. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `samePayment`)
- A later settlement the tool itself asked for is checked again and replaces planted content with the tool's own answer to its listed call; a planted settlement can neither change nor withdraw a confirmed tool whose listed call answers with a PaymentRequired or fails for now. Once that call is answered without one, the first call a payer proposed, largest payments first, whose PaymentRequired lists the tool for its owner updates the tool with the tool's own answer to that call, and the example that answer declares becomes the listed call (the payer's arguments when the tool echoes them; the change then stays until that call is answered without a PaymentRequired). ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `checkOrigins`)
- A check calls the tool as its listing describes first; the example arguments its settlements proposed are tried only when that call is answered without a PaymentRequired (a published tool whose listed call fails for now is retried instead), and only a PaymentRequired to them that lists the tool for its owner is used, so echoed arguments can confirm a tool but never withdraw it: only the listed call's answer does. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `originAsker`)
- Domain trust is granted only to origin-verified listings, and needs the two-way SEP-1 binding. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `enrich`)
- The owner's hourly quota is kept per origin, and only listings that origin confirmed for a settlement that paid the owner its price count toward it. ([`packages/store-postgres/src/catalog-store.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/store-postgres/src/catalog-store.ts), `countCreatedBy`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never publishes a tool description or price planted with
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a planted settlement withdraw or change a tool its origin confirmed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a planted settlement withdraw a tool waiting for the check its owner's buyer asked for
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a dust settlement discard the payment a tool's real buyer made
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lists a tool from its buyer's call, whatever call a larger payment proposed that its server fails
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets a payload's proposed call fail the check of a tool that answers its listed call
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): updates an MCP tool's metadata once its origin confirms the change
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): lets a domain vouch for an MCP tool listed under its host
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): does not charge an owner's quota for listings other payers created and its origin never confirmed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): change or withdraw a tool whose listed call fails for now
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): updates a published tool from its owner's proposed call only once its listed call no longer gets a 402

**Conformance runs.** `live-bazaar` case `mcp-tool`.

**Residual risk.** Tools under an mcp:// URL (P10). A planted settlement can quarantine a tool that was never confirmed until the tool's next real settlement. Once a published tool's listed call is answered without a PaymentRequired (its arguments changed), the first call a payer proposed, largest payments first, whose PaymentRequired lists the tool for its owner updates it with the tool's own answer to that call, which for a tool that prices by its arguments may differ from its answer to other calls.

### P10. Unverifiable content of mcp:// tools

Accepted risk · Spoofing, Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A tool under an mcp:// URL names no server, so nothing but the settlement vouches for its description, schema and price; whoever first pays its owner for it chooses them.

**What Stellar makes different.** As P9: a settlement proves the payment, not the content.

**Preconditions.** One settlement of one base unit to the owner, before the tool's first real settlement. **Impact.** A misleading description or price on the owner's mcp:// tool listing.

**Mitigations**

- mcp:// tools are scoped to their owner and published at trust settled, which they never leave: no domain can vouch for them. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `verifiable`)
- The Rail402 SDK does not call mcp:// resources, and its default pubnet policy pays MCP listings only at domain_verified. ([`packages/sdk/src/policy/policy.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/sdk/src/policy/policy.ts), `resolvePolicy`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): publishes an MCP tool under an mcp:// URL at once, at trust settled, keeping its first content

**Residual risk.** On testnet, the first content of an mcp:// tool is chosen by its first payer and kept, and such tools, counted apart, can fill their half of the network's quota and room. Sellers who want their own content listed serve their tools at an http(s) endpoint. Pubnet refuses mcp:// tools (P11).

### P11. Buyer-written mcp:// tools on pubnet

Mitigated · Spoofing, Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A tool under an mcp:// URL would be published at once with content chosen by whoever first pays its owner one base unit; on pubnet that content reaches agents paying real money.

**What Stellar makes different.** A pubnet settlement of one base unit with a sponsored fee costs the attacker almost nothing.

**Preconditions.** One pubnet settlement to the owner carrying a bazaar extension for an mcp:// tool. **Impact.** Without protection: an impostor tool description or price shown to pubnet buyers.

**Mitigations**

- Pubnet refuses mcp:// tools before any write with bazaar_resource_unverifiable, at /verify and /settle, reported in EXTENSION-RESPONSES; MCP tools at an https endpoint are cataloged through their origin check. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `refusal`)
- Pubnet rules cannot be loosened: PUBNET_BAZAAR_ORIGINLESS_MCP accepts only refuse, and the catalog refuses on pubnet whatever it is given. ([`packages/bazaar/src/policy.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/policy.ts), `catalogRulesFor`)
- An optional minimum settled amount per asset and network makes each pubnet listing cost what the operator chooses. ([`apps/rail402/src/discovery-config.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/discovery-config.ts), `parseMinimums`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): refuses an mcp:// tool on pubnet and keeps it on testnet
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): refuses an http resource URL on pubnet
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): refuses a settlement below the network's minimum for its asset
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts): refuses mcp:// tools on pubnet and refuses a pubnet setting that would publish them

**Residual risk.** None on pubnet; testnet keeps P10.

### P12. A token named USDC treated as US dollars

Mitigated · Tampering · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A dollar price filter that recognised US-dollar assets by symbol would treat any accepted token whose symbol is USDC as dollars, so a listing priced in a worthless token would match a buyer's dollar ceiling.

**What Stellar makes different.** Asset symbols are not unique on Stellar; only the issuer, or the contract address, identifies a token.

**Preconditions.** An operator accepts a second token that uses a dollar stablecoin's symbol. **Impact.** Without protection: search results that misstate what a buyer pays in dollars.

**Mitigations**

- US-dollar assets are chosen by contract (Circle's USDC per network by default, or &lt;NET&gt;\_BAZAAR_USD_ASSETS), never by symbol. ([`apps/rail402/src/discovery-config.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/discovery-config.ts), `isUsdAsset`)
- A listing's price comes from its origin's 402, never from what the buyer echoed. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `verdict`)

**Tests**

- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts): does not treat a token named USDC from another issuer as USD
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts): treats only configured asset contracts as US dollars, never a symbol
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): publishes a price change the origin confirms, with a public version history

**Residual risk.** A seller can still price its own resource as it likes.

### P13. Pre-empting a pubnet seller's listing

Mitigated · Spoofing · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** An attacker pays a known seller's payTo first, with echoed metadata, to plant a pending listing or burn that owner's hourly quota before the seller lists, for example with listings on its own host that answers a 402 naming the seller's payTo, or that it hands over to the seller.

**What Stellar makes different.** Anyone can pay any account that can receive the asset.

**Preconditions.** One settlement per planted listing to the seller's payTo. **Impact.** Without protection: the seller's own listings refused for the hour, or planted content shown.

**Mitigations**

- A pending listing is never listed or searched until its origin names the payTo; its public detail and history show its state and settled payment but withhold the metadata the payer echoed. Only listings the owner's origin confirmed count toward its quota, and the network-wide quota counts published listings only, when they are first published. ([`packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts), `withheldContent`)
- The owner's quota is kept per origin (scheme, host and port), so listings an attacker's host confirms for the payTo count only at that host and never against the seller's own origins. ([`packages/bazaar/src/store.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/store.ts), `resourceOrigin`)
- A listing counts toward its owner's quota only once its origin confirmed a settlement that paid the owner its price there (for upto, the whole cap), and never after an ownership transfer; none is charged past the quota, and one that would be waits to be published. Dust payments for spellings of a seller's route that its server answers alike (case, repeated or trailing slashes), and listings handed over to the seller, cannot use up its quota. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `quotaCharge`)
- Quotas are kept per network, so testnet payments never burn a seller's pubnet quota. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `budgetFor`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): does not charge an owner's quota for listings other payers created and its origin never confirmed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): keeps each payer's and each owner's new-listing quota per network
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): keeps a payTo's quota per origin, so listings another host confirms for it never block its own
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets listings moved to a payTo on another host use up its quota on its own host
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): counts a payTo's new listings per scheme, host and port, a route template at its host
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): charges a payTo's quota only for listings a settlement paid its origin's price for
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never charges a payTo's quota for listings handed over to it at its own origin
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): publishes no more of a payTo's new listings at an origin per hour than its quota, however many are confirmed at once
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery.test.ts): show their state and settled payment, never the metadata a payer echoed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never lets unconfirmed listings use up the network-wide quota, so dust payments cannot block a seller

**Residual risk.** An attacker can delay an owner's listing by one origin check; claims are kept, so paying again does not extend the delay. A party that pays a seller its full price for each of the quota's listings in an hour, for example for spellings of one route the seller's server answers alike, still uses up the seller's quota at that origin: that is buying from the seller, and each spelling is listed separately. One host shared by several sellers is one origin, so a tenant that sets its own routes' payTo can do the same there at its own price.

### P14. Origin checks reflected at third-party hosts and trust shown across networks

Mitigated · Denial of service, Spoofing · boundary TB5 (Facilitator and the open internet) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** A per-process origin-check budget lets testnet payments make the service send requests to public hosts at pubnet's expense; an item-level trust that is the maximum over networks lets a pubnet buyer read trust earned on testnet; and a settlement count summed over networks shows free testnet settlements next to a pubnet offer.

**What Stellar makes different.** Trust is proved per network: a home_domain and a settlement exist on one network only.

**Preconditions.** Listings for one resource on both networks, or many testnet listings on one host. **Impact.** Without protection: pubnet origin checks delayed by testnet traffic; a pubnet listing shown as domain_verified on testnet's proof.

**Mitigations**

- Origin requests per host are budgeted per network. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `spendHostBudget`)
- Origin checks are claimed and run in one lane per network, a bounded number at a time; a network whose lane is busy is skipped, so unanswering testnet hosts never delay pubnet checks. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `checkOrigins`)
- An item shows the weakest of its listings' trust, and with a network filter that network's listing only. ([`packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts), `trustFor`)
- An item's settlement count is the fewest of its listings', never a sum across networks; each network's count is shown on its own listing. ([`packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts), `settlementsFor`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): paces origin requests to one host per network, so testnet checks never spend pubnet's host budget
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): checks each network's origins in its own lane, so a testnet backlog of unanswering hosts never delays pubnet
- [`packages/bazaar/test/wire.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/wire.test.ts): shows pubnet trust only from pubnet proof
- [`packages/bazaar/test/wire.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/wire.test.ts): never adds testnet settlements to a pubnet count
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery.integration.test.ts): shows each network's trust, never trust proved on another network

**Residual risk.** Testnet still causes bounded outbound requests.

### P15. A payer decides whether a seller's usage is published

Mitigated · Tampering, Information disclosure · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Catalog and search integrity

**Threat.** The private-usage opt-out is read from the payload a buyer echoes. For a listing no origin confirms, such as a tool under an mcp:// URL, the first payer's echo would decide it for good: leaving the key out publishes every later payer of a seller that opted out; adding it hides a seller's usage.

**What Stellar makes different.** A testnet settlement costs nothing, and the smallest pubnet one almost nothing.

**Preconditions.** One settlement to the seller whose payload omits or adds rail402-private-usage. **Impact.** Without protection: payer addresses, transactions and amounts of a seller that opted out published by the usage routes and the Explorer.

**Mitigations**

- Usage is never published for a listing whose content no origin confirmed (trust settled): the usage routes answer discovery_usage_private or leave it out, whatever its first payer echoed. The rule is applied at read time, so listings cataloged before it are covered without changing their data. ([`packages/bazaar/src/store.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/store.ts), `export function usageVisible`)
- The same rule in the Postgres store's usage queries. ([`packages/store-postgres/src/catalog-store.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/store-postgres/src/catalog-store.ts), `const PRIVATE_USAGE`)
- The opt-out ratchets one way: a settlement whose payload declares it makes the usage private at once and asks the origin to confirm; nothing a payer sends removes it, only the origin's own 402 does. ([`packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/catalog.ts), `const ratchet`)

**Tests**

- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts): stays private for an mcp:// tool whatever its first payer echoed, and links no settlement
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts): keeps a declared opt-out once any settlement declared it, whatever later payers echo
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): never publishes the usage of an mcp:// tool, whose content no origin confirmed
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): makes an mcp:// tool's usage private once a settlement declares it, never public again
- [`packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/testing.ts): makes a listing's usage private when a payload declares it, until its origin says otherwise

**Residual risk.** A payer can still hide an origin-confirmed listing's usage until its origin check runs again; that fails private, never public.

### P16. The usage routes publish what each payer bought

Mitigated · Information disclosure · boundary TB2 (Seller and facilitator) · asset: Payer privacy

**Threat.** The usage routes link settlements, and with them payer addresses, to the listings they paid for, keylessly; an explorer caching them shows each payer's purchases labelled by resource. On pubnet these are real customers' purchase histories.

**What Stellar makes different.** Payments are public on chain, but name only payer, payTo and amount; the resource bought is known only to the seller and the catalog.

**Preconditions.** Any reader of the public discovery routes. **Impact.** Without protection: a pubnet payer's purchases published by resource, without the payer's consent.

**Mitigations**

- Each network publishes either settlement links or counts only (&lt;NET&gt;\_BAZAAR_USAGE_DETAIL); pubnet defaults to counts: /discovery/settlements links nothing and a listing's usage lists no settlement or payer. ([`apps/rail402/src/discovery.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/discovery.ts), `export function defaultUsageDetail`)
- The setting is read per network, so testnet's never applies to pubnet. ([`apps/rail402/src/discovery-config.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/discovery-config.ts), `BAZAAR_USAGE_DETAIL`)
- The Explorer shows a payment's 'paid for' only from the Bazaar's links, so with counts it labels no pubnet payment and says the network publishes counts only. ([`apps/explorer/src/web/pages/resource.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/explorer/src/web/pages/resource.ts), `publishes payment counts only`)
- The other public routes keep the same promise: a listing's version history names no settlement transaction while its usage is private or its network publishes counts only, and a private listing shows no lastSettled. ([`packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/wire.ts), `export function toVersionItem`)

**Tests**

- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts): with counts, links no settlement to a listing and lists no payer, but still counts
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts): publishes counts only on pubnet by default, settlements on testnet
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-config.test.ts): publishes usage counts only on pubnet by default, each network settable on its own
- [`apps/explorer/test/explorer-fixes.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/explorer/test/explorer-fixes.test.ts): says a network publishes payment counts only, and links no payment to the listing then
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts): links no version to a settlement, and dates no settlement, while the usage is private
- [`apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/discovery-usage.test.ts): links no version to a settlement on a network that publishes usage counts only

**Residual risk.** An operator can set PUBNET_BAZAAR_USAGE_DETAIL=settlements; counts and dates stay public and can still be matched to a seller's few on-chain payments. Testnet publishes links by default. A private listing's settlement count stays public; polling it shows when the listing was bought.

{/* generated:end */}

## SSRF

Cataloging makes the facilitator fetch URLs that sellers choose, and paying makes an agent fetch URLs that a
listing or a prompt suggests. Both run through a fetch that resolves the host itself, refuses non-public
addresses at dial time, follows no redirect it has not re-checked, and caps sizes and times.

{/* generated:begin topic-ssrf */}

### X1. Origin checks to private or metadata addresses

Mitigated · Information disclosure, Elevation of privilege · boundary TB5 (Facilitator and the open internet) · asset: Operator infrastructure

**Threat.** A listing's resource URL points at a private, loopback, link-local or cloud metadata address, so the catalog's origin check reaches the operator's internal network.

**What Stellar makes different.** Cataloging runs after any settlement, so anyone who can pay a cent can make the facilitator fetch a URL.

**Preconditions.** One settlement with a crafted resource URL. **Impact.** Requests from the facilitator's network position; internal data in listing metadata.

**Mitigations**

- Hosts are resolved and every address checked against a block list at dial time, IP literals before any lookup. ([`packages/bazaar/src/origin.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/origin.ts), `safeLookup`)
- Private and loopback addresses are refused unless DISCOVERY_ALLOW_LOOPBACK is set, which is refused together with pubnet. ([`apps/rail402/src/config.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/src/config.ts), `DISCOVERY_ALLOW_LOOPBACK`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/test/origin.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/origin.test.ts): refuses a host whose DNS answers are not public, before connecting
- [`packages/bazaar/test/egress.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/egress.test.ts): refuses a private IP literal for origin checks unless loopback is allowed
- [`packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/security.test.ts): classifies public and non-public addresses
- [`apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/apps/rail402/test/config.test.ts): refuses loopback discovery fetches on a service that serves pubnet

**Residual risk.** None known. The RPC URL is operator configuration and trusted.

### X2. DNS rebinding

Mitigated · Information disclosure, Elevation of privilege · boundary TB5 (Facilitator and the open internet) · asset: Operator infrastructure

**Threat.** A hostname answers with a public address when checked and a private one when connected.

**What Stellar makes different.** The SEP-1 domain check fetches stellar.toml from a host the seller names, so a seller's own DNS zone is the natural rebinding vector. That fetch uses the same pinned lookup as origin checks.

**Preconditions.** Control of a DNS zone. **Impact.** As X1.

**Mitigations**

- The address that is checked is the address that is dialled: the lookup used by the connection validates every answer. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/lookup.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/lookup.ts), `pinnedLookup`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts): checks every DNS answer, so a rebinding name cannot reach a private address
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/address.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/address.test.ts): refuses an empty answer and a mixed one, without naming the address

**Residual risk.** None.

### X3. Redirects to internal hosts

Mitigated · Information disclosure, Elevation of privilege · boundary TB5 (Facilitator and the open internet) · asset: Operator infrastructure

**Threat.** A public origin answers with a redirect to a private address.

**What Stellar makes different.** Rail402 reads stellar.toml only from the listing's own host and follows no redirect for it, so a seller cannot point the SEP-1 check at an internal address.

**Preconditions.** Control of a public origin. **Impact.** As X1.

**Mitigations**

- Origin checks and SEP-1 fetches follow no redirects; buyers follow only same-origin redirects, re-checking each hop, and none after paying. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/redirect.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/redirect.ts), `nextHop`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/test/origin.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/origin.test.ts): does not follow redirects
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts): never follows a redirect towards a private or other destination
- [`packages/sdk/test/http-flow.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/sdk/test/http-flow.test.ts): never follows a redirect after paying

**Conformance runs.** `rejection-audit` case `mcp-redirect-private`.

**Residual risk.** None.

### X4. Oversized or slow answers

Mitigated · Denial of service · boundary TB5 (Facilitator and the open internet) · asset: Availability

**Threat.** An origin answers with a huge, compressed or endless body, or holds connections open by trickling bytes or by never reading the request body.

**What Stellar makes different.** SEP-1 limits stellar.toml to 100 KB; Rail402 enforces that limit on the decoded file.

**Preconditions.** Control of an origin. **Impact.** Memory exhaustion or stalled workers.

**Mitigations**

- Body and header caps apply after decoding, with header, idle and total timeouts. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/limits.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/limits.ts), `DEFAULT_LIMITS`)
- A body safe-fetch does not return (a redirect's, refused or followed, or a 205's) is never read: unless it arrived whole with the headers and the whole request was sent, its connection is closed at once and never returns to the pool, so an endless redirect body cannot hold a connection past the request. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts), `function discard`)
- An answer that ends before the server took the whole request body closes its connection, instead of leaving it, with no timeout, to an upload the server may never read. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts), `Such a connection cannot be reused: close it.`)
- Nothing past the end of a compressed body is read: an answer that goes on after it (bytes after a deflate or br stream, gzip padding) closes its connection once the body is read, so those bytes are neither left uncounted nor watched only by an idle timeout they keep resetting. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts), `connection unless the answer itself has ended`)
- Origin checks close the connection as soon as the response headers arrive and never read a body; stellar.toml reads keep one deadline through the whole body and close the socket on every outcome. ([`packages/bazaar/src/origin.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/origin.ts), `close the connection now`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts): stops oversized and compressed-bomb answers at the decoded limit
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): times out waiting for headers, on an idle body, and in total
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes a refused redirect instead of reading its body, and frees its connection
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes a followed redirect's endless body before the next hop
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes a 205 answer that sends a body anyway, and frees its connection
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes a refused redirect's connection and frees its slot
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes a followed redirect's connection before the next hop
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes the connection once the answer is read
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/fetch.test.ts): closes the connection when a compressed body ends before the answer
- [`packages/bazaar/test/stellar-toml.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/stellar-toml.test.ts): refuses a file larger than SEP-1 allows
- [`packages/bazaar/test/origin.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/origin.test.ts): closes the connection once the headers arrive, so a drip-fed body never holds the socket
- [`packages/bazaar/test/stellar-toml.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/stellar-toml.test.ts): keeps one deadline through the body, so a drip-fed file is cut off and its socket closed

**Residual risk.** None.

### X5. stellar.toml fetches

Mitigated · Information disclosure · boundary TB5 (Facilitator and the open internet) · asset: Operator infrastructure

**Threat.** The domain check fetches /.well-known/stellar.toml from a host the seller chose.

**What Stellar makes different.** SEP-1 lets a domain claim Stellar accounts; Rail402 reads ACCOUNTS, and the owner's home_domain, to raise an origin-verified listing's trust to domain_verified only when both name each other, which means fetching from the seller's host.

**Preconditions.** One listed resource. **Impact.** As X1.

**Mitigations**

- The SEP-1 fetch uses the same dial-time address check and size cap as origin checks. ([`packages/bazaar/src/stellar-toml.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/src/stellar-toml.ts), `fetchStellarTomlAccounts`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/bazaar/test/stellar-toml.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/stellar-toml.test.ts): never connects to a private address unless loopback is explicitly allowed
- [`packages/bazaar/test/egress.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/bazaar/test/egress.test.ts): refuses a private IP literal for stellar.toml

**Residual risk.** None.

### X6. Paid calls to agent-chosen URLs

Mitigated · Information disclosure, Elevation of privilege · boundary TB6 (Agent, MCP server and paid URLs) · asset: Agent budgets

**Threat.** An agent, possibly steered by text in a listing, asks pay_and_call (or the SDK or CLI) to call a private address, sending a payment or the agent's request there.

**What Stellar makes different.** None: the buyer's egress runs on the agent's machine.

**Preconditions.** An agent that follows URLs from untrusted content. **Impact.** Requests to the agent's internal network, with a payment header attached.

**Mitigations**

- Every buyer request goes through safe-fetch: public destinations only, the address pinned at dial time, redirects re-checked. ([`packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/src/fetch.ts), `createSafeFetch`)

**Tests**

- [`packages/sdk/test/http-flow.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/sdk/test/http-flow.test.ts): refuses private destinations before sending anything
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts): refuses IP-literal URLs to this machine in every spelling, before connecting
- [`packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts`](https://github.com/tolgayayci/rail402/blob/main/packages/safe-fetch/test/egress.integration.test.ts): refuses a name that resolves to a private address, without naming the address

**Conformance runs.** `rejection-audit` case `mcp-private-address`.

**Residual risk.** None.

{/* generated:end */}

## Smart accounts

A `C…` payer is a contract account whose `__check_auth` decides whether a payment is authorized. Nothing about
it can be checked offline: only executing it tells, and its answer can change between simulation and
inclusion. Rail402 simulates in enforcing mode at verify and again at settle; policy composition with `upto`
is proved in contract tests with OpenZeppelin smart accounts.

{/* generated:begin topic-smart-accounts */}

### A1. A payment signed by a key the account does not accept

Mitigated · Spoofing · boundary TB7 (Contracts) · asset: Payer funds

**Threat.** Someone signs a C-account payment with a key that is not one of the smart account's signers.

**What Stellar makes different.** A contract account authorizes through its own \_\_check_auth; only executing it can tell whether a signature is valid. There is no signature a facilitator could check offline.

**Preconditions.** Knowledge of the smart account's address. **Impact.** None if detected; the account's \_\_check_auth refuses.
